diff --git a/scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts b/scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..b271ea5ce07287638c8b1be7d29133c72fa0d72b
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,118 @@
+/**
+ * Rebuilds the transparent 3D logo exports with `npm run generate-logo`.
+ *
+ * The front outlines are measured from public/logo/promptbook-logo-blue-transparent-1024.png.
+ * Its three disconnected pieces and the B counter are kept intact. Each face is extruded along its own
+ * isometric normal; material gradients and a narrow rim supply the lighting. Blue and white use the same geometry.
+ * Only this build-time script draws the artwork; pages and downloads share its static SVG and PNG files.
+ */
+import { writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import sharp from 'sharp';
+import { PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS, type PromptbookLogoTone } from '../lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets';
+
+const VIEW_BOX_SIZE = 1024;
+const DEPTH_STEPS = 36;
+const OUTPUT_DIRECTORY = path.resolve(process.cwd(), 'public');
+
+const LOGO_FACES = [
+    {
+        id: 'top',
+        outline: 'M60 283 467 47V283Z',
+        depth: [0, 32],
+    },
+    {
+        id: 'p',
+        outline:
+            'M184 395C181 393 179 395 179 401V768C179 775 180 779 187 783L222 802' +
+            'C224 803 226 803 226 800V638L274 666C311 688 335 672 346 642' +
+            'C354 619 354 582 346 548C336 505 312 469 279 448Z',
+        depth: [28, -16],
+    },
+    {
+        id: 'b',
+        outline:
+            'M470 520 878 285V755L470 989Z' +
+            'M588 498V878L661 836C723 801 760 746 760 675' +
+            'C760 636 748 609 719 605C744 576 757 542 757 501' +
+            'C757 456 741 421 705 438L592 492C589 494 588 495 588 498Z',
+        depth: [-28, -16],
+    },
+] as const;
+
+const MATERIALS = {
+    blue: {
+        top: ['#797bff', '#3434be', '#141077'],
+        p: ['#494cdb', '#211a9d', '#090548'],
+        b: ['#5b5bea', '#2420ac', '#09045b'],
+        side: ['#343598', '#080431'],
+        rim: ['#cedcff', '#777cea', '#272073'],
+    },
+    white: {
+        top: ['#ffffff', '#edf6ff', '#b5cde4'],
+        p: ['#f1f8ff', '#cfdfef', '#94acc9'],
+        b: ['#ffffff', '#e2effa', '#b7cfe5'],
+        side: ['#93aecb', '#4d6684'],
+        rim: ['#ffffff', '#ffffff', '#a8c6df'],
+    },
+} as const;
+
+function createGradient(id: string, colors: readonly string[]): string {
+    const stops = colors.map((color, index) =>
+        `<stop offset="${index / (colors.length - 1)}" stop-color="${color}"/>`,
+    ).join('');
+
+    return `<linearGradient id="${id}" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0.8" y2="1">${stops}</linearGradient>`;
+}
+
+function createFace({ id, depth }: (typeof LOGO_FACES)[number]): string {
+    const layers = Array.from({ length: DEPTH_STEPS }, (_, index) => {
+        const fraction = (DEPTH_STEPS - index) / DEPTH_STEPS;
+        const horizontal = (depth[0] * fraction).toFixed(2);
+        const vertical = (depth[1] * fraction).toFixed(2);
+
+        return `<use href="#${id}" transform="translate(${horizontal} ${vertical})"/>`;
+    }).join('');
+
+    return `<g fill="url(#side)">${layers}</g>` +
+        `<use href="#${id}" fill="url(#${id}-face)"/>` +
+        `<use href="#${id}" fill="none" stroke="url(#rim)" stroke-width="3" stroke-linejoin="round"/>`;
+}
+
+function createLogoSvg(tone: PromptbookLogoTone): string {
+    const material = MATERIALS[tone];
+    const outlines = LOGO_FACES.map(({ id, outline }) => `<path id="${id}" d="${outline}"/>`).join('');
+    const faceGradients = LOGO_FACES.map(({ id }) => createGradient(`${id}-face`, material[id])).join('');
+
+    return '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n' +
+        '<!-- Generated by npm run generate-logo. Edit scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts. -->\n' +
+        `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${VIEW_BOX_SIZE}" height="${VIEW_BOX_SIZE}"` +
+        ` viewBox="0 0 ${VIEW_BOX_SIZE} ${VIEW_BOX_SIZE}" fill-rule="evenodd">` +
+        '<title>Promptbook 3D logo</title>' +
+        `<defs>${outlines}${faceGradients}${createGradient('side', material.side)}` +
+        `${createGradient('rim', material.rim)}</defs>${LOGO_FACES.map(createFace).join('')}</svg>\n`;
+}
+
+async function writeLogoAssets(tone: PromptbookLogoTone): Promise<void> {
+    const assets = PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS[tone];
+    const svg = createLogoSvg(tone);
+
+    await writeFile(path.join(OUTPUT_DIRECTORY, assets.svg), svg);
+    for (const [size, destination] of [[256, assets.png256], [1024, assets.png1024]] as const) {
+        await sharp(Buffer.from(svg), { density: 288 })
+            .resize(size, size)
+            .png()
+            .toFile(path.join(OUTPUT_DIRECTORY, destination));
+    }
+}
+
+async function main(): Promise<void> {
+    await writeLogoAssets('blue');
+    await writeLogoAssets('white');
+    console.info('Generated blue and white 3D Promptbook logos (SVG, 256 PNG, 1024 PNG).');
+}
+
+main().catch((error: unknown) => {
+    console.error(error);
+    process.exitCode = 1;
+});

/bin/zsh -lc "cat > .tmp/promptbook-logo-review/check.mjs <<'JAVASCRIPT'
import { chromium } from '@playwright/test';
import { writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import sharp from 'sharp';

const BROWSER = await chromium.launch();
const PAGE = await BROWSER.newPage({ viewport: { width: 1440, height: 1000 }, reducedMotion: 'reduce' });
const PAGE_ERRORS = [];
const RESULTS = [];
PAGE.on('pageerror', error => PAGE_ERRORS.push(error.message));
try {
    for (const route of ['/cs', '/en', '/branding', '/dekujeme']) {
        const response = await PAGE.goto("'`http://127.0.0.1:4009${route}`, { waitUntil: '"'networkidle', timeout: 180000 });
        const images = PAGE.locator('img[src*=\"promptbook-logo-3d\"]');
        for (const image of await images.all()) {
            await image.scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
            await image.evaluate(element => element.decode());
        }
        await PAGE.evaluate(() => window.scrollTo(0, 0));
        await PAGE.waitForTimeout(1000);
        const imageDetails = await images.evaluateAll(elements => elements.map(element => ({src: element.getAttribute('src'), isLoaded: element.complete && element.naturalWidth > 0, width: element.width, height: element.height})));
        const isOverflowing = await PAGE.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.scrollWidth > window.innerWidth);
        if ("'!response?.ok() || imageDetails.some(image => !image.isLoaded) || isOverflowing) throw new Error(`Logo page check failed: ${route} ${JSON.stringify(imageDetails)}`);
        RESULTS.push({ route, viewport: '"'desktop', images: imageDetails, isOverflowing });
        await PAGE.screenshot({ path: "'`.tmp/promptbook-logo-review/${route.slice(1)}-desktop.png` });
        if (route === '"'/branding') {
            await PAGE.getByRole('heading', {name:'Pick the logo that fits the background.'}).scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
            await PAGE.screenshot({ path: '.tmp/promptbook-logo-review/branding-downloads.png' });
            const downloadPaths = await PAGE.locator('a[download][href*=\"promptbook-logo-3d\"]').evaluateAll(elements => [...new Set(elements.map(element => element.getAttribute('href')))]);
            for (const downloadPath of downloadPaths) {
                const downloadResponse = await PAGE.request.get("'`http://127.0.0.1:4009${downloadPath}`);
                if (!downloadResponse.ok()) throw new Error(`Missing download: ${downloadPath}`);
                const metadata = await sharp(await downloadResponse.body()).metadata();
                if (!metadata.hasAlpha || metadata.width !== metadata.height) throw new Error(`Invalid transparent export: ${downloadPath}`);
                RESULTS.push({ downloadPath, format: metadata.format, size: metadata.width, hasAlpha: metadata.hasAlpha });
            }
            const downloadEvent = PAGE.waitForEvent('"'download');
            await PAGE.getByRole('link', { name: 'Download the 3D logo', exact: true }).click();
            const download = await downloadEvent;
            RESULTS.push({ downloadedFilename: download.suggestedFilename(), downloadFailure: await download.failure() });
        }
        if (route === '/cs') {
            await PAGE.locator('footer img[src*=\"promptbook-logo-3d-white\"]').scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
            await PAGE.screenshot({ path: '.tmp/promptbook-logo-review/footer-desktop.png' });
        }
    }
    await PAGE.setViewportSize({ width: 390, height: 844 });
    for (const route of ['/cs', '/en', '/branding', '/dekujeme']) {
        await PAGE.goto("'`http://127.0.0.1:4009${route}`, { waitUntil: '"'networkidle', timeout: 180000 });
        await PAGE.waitForTimeout(1000);
        const isOverflowing = await PAGE.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.scrollWidth > window.innerWidth);
        if (isOverflowing) throw new Error("'`Mobile overflow: ${route}`);
        RESULTS.push({ route, viewport: '"'mobile', isOverflowing });
        await PAGE.screenshot({ path: "'`.tmp/promptbook-logo-review/${route.slice(1)}-mobile.png` });
    }
    const STATIC_PAGE = await BROWSER.newPage({ javaScriptEnabled: false, viewport: { width: 1440, height: 1000 } });
    await STATIC_PAGE.goto('"'http://127.0.0.1:4009/branding', { waitUntil: 'load' });
    const IS_STATIC_LOGO_LOADED = await STATIC_PAGE.locator('img[src*=\"promptbook-logo-3d\"]').first().evaluate(element => element.complete && element.naturalWidth > 0);
    if ("'!IS_STATIC_LOGO_LOADED) throw new Error('"'Logo does not render without JavaScript');
    RESULTS.push({ isStaticLogoLoaded: IS_STATIC_LOGO_LOADED, pageErrors: PAGE_ERRORS });
    await writeFile('.tmp/promptbook-logo-review/results.json', JSON.stringify(RESULTS, null, 2));
    console.log(JSON.stringify(RESULTS, null, 2));
    if (PAGE_ERRORS.length) process.exitCode = 1;
} finally {
    await BROWSER.close();
}

diff --git a/README.md b/README.md
index edf7257a302096b486cd1cc6bba29c38d579ac24..c9a652e6c818ec60446911b94bac11703fcc339e
--- a/README.md
+++ b/README.md
@@ -71,6 +71,18 @@
 
 ## Technology
 
+### Promptbook logo
+
+The shared `PromptbookLogo` component uses a static 3D SVG, with a blue material for light surfaces and a white
+material for dark ones. The same artwork is available on `/branding` as SVG and transparent 256/1024-pixel PNGs,
+alongside the original flat logos. It needs no animation or WebGL runtime.
+
+Edit the measured outlines, extrusion and lighting in `scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts`, then run
+`npm run generate-logo` and commit the exports in `public/logo/`. Their public paths are shared through
+`lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets.ts`; the generator is an explicit development step, not part of page rendering.
+
+### Stack
+
 - **Next.js 15** App Router, React, and TypeScript
 - **Tailwind CSS** and Radix-based reusable UI components
 - **PostgreSQL** migrations and server-side access through `pg`

diff --git a/components/promptbook-logo.tsx b/components/promptbook-logo.tsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..2fcc49415664fbcd1a6937b83fe05f5a60cf5000
--- /dev/null
+++ b/components/promptbook-logo.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,33 @@
+import { PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS, type PromptbookLogoTone } from '@/lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets';
+import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
+import Image from 'next/image';
+
+interface PromptbookLogoProps {
+    /** The white material keeps its shading on dark backgrounds. */
+    tone?: PromptbookLogoTone;
+    size?: number;
+    className?: string;
+    /** Use an empty label when adjacent text already names Promptbook. */
+    alt?: string;
+    isPriority?: boolean;
+}
+
+/** The shared 3D mark is an ordinary image: crisp at any size, with no canvas, motion, or hydration dependency. */
+export function PromptbookLogo({
+    tone = 'blue',
+    size = 32,
+    className,
+    alt = 'Promptbook',
+    isPriority = false,
+}: PromptbookLogoProps) {
+    return (
+        <Image
+            src={PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS[tone].svg}
+            alt={alt}
+            width={size}
+            height={size}
+            className={cn('shrink-0 object-contain', className)}
+            priority={isPriority}
+        />
+    );
+}
diff --git a/lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets.ts b/lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..c30c10be5fdaf7dc4aed32868a859232e41adae3
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,15 @@
+/** Static exports of the same 3D artwork, generated by scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts. */
+export const PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS = {
+    blue: {
+        svg: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-blue.svg',
+        png256: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-blue-256.png',
+        png1024: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-blue-1024.png',
+    },
+    white: {
+        svg: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-white.svg',
+        png256: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-white-256.png',
+        png1024: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-white-1024.png',
+    },
+} as const;
+
+export type PromptbookLogoTone = keyof typeof PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS;

diff --git a/scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts b/scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..b271ea5ce07287638c8b1be7d29133c72fa0d72b
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,118 @@
+/**
+ * Rebuilds the transparent 3D logo exports with `npm run generate-logo`.
+ *
+ * The front outlines are measured from public/logo/promptbook-logo-blue-transparent-1024.png.
+ * Its three disconnected pieces and the B counter are kept intact. Each face is extruded along its own
+ * isometric normal; material gradients and a narrow rim supply the lighting. Blue and white use the same geometry.
+ * Only this build-time script draws the artwork; pages and downloads share its static SVG and PNG files.
+ */
+import { writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import sharp from 'sharp';
+import { PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS, type PromptbookLogoTone } from '../lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets';
+
+const VIEW_BOX_SIZE = 1024;
+const DEPTH_STEPS = 36;
+const OUTPUT_DIRECTORY = path.resolve(process.cwd(), 'public');
+
+const LOGO_FACES = [
+    {
+        id: 'top',
+        outline: 'M60 283 467 47V283Z',
+        depth: [0, 32],
+    },
+    {
+        id: 'p',
+        outline:
+            'M184 395C181 393 179 395 179 401V768C179 775 180 779 187 783L222 802' +
+            'C224 803 226 803 226 800V638L274 666C311 688 335 672 346 642' +
+            'C354 619 354 582 346 548C336 505 312 469 279 448Z',
+        depth: [28, -16],
+    },
+    {
+        id: 'b',
+        outline:
+            'M470 520 878 285V755L470 989Z' +
+            'M588 498V878L661 836C723 801 760 746 760 675' +
+            'C760 636 748 609 719 605C744 576 757 542 757 501' +
+            'C757 456 741 421 705 438L592 492C589 494 588 495 588 498Z',
+        depth: [-28, -16],
+    },
+] as const;
+
+const MATERIALS = {
+    blue: {
+        top: ['#797bff', '#3434be', '#141077'],
+        p: ['#494cdb', '#211a9d', '#090548'],
+        b: ['#5b5bea', '#2420ac', '#09045b'],
+        side: ['#343598', '#080431'],
+        rim: ['#cedcff', '#777cea', '#272073'],
+    },
+    white: {
+        top: ['#ffffff', '#edf6ff', '#b5cde4'],
+        p: ['#f1f8ff', '#cfdfef', '#94acc9'],
+        b: ['#ffffff', '#e2effa', '#b7cfe5'],
+        side: ['#93aecb', '#4d6684'],
+        rim: ['#ffffff', '#ffffff', '#a8c6df'],
+    },
+} as const;
+
+function createGradient(id: string, colors: readonly string[]): string {
+    const stops = colors.map((color, index) =>
+        `<stop offset="${index / (colors.length - 1)}" stop-color="${color}"/>`,
+    ).join('');
+
+    return `<linearGradient id="${id}" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0.8" y2="1">${stops}</linearGradient>`;
+}
+
+function createFace({ id, depth }: (typeof LOGO_FACES)[number]): string {
+    const layers = Array.from({ length: DEPTH_STEPS }, (_, index) => {
+        const fraction = (DEPTH_STEPS - index) / DEPTH_STEPS;
+        const horizontal = (depth[0] * fraction).toFixed(2);
+        const vertical = (depth[1] * fraction).toFixed(2);
+
+        return `<use href="#${id}" transform="translate(${horizontal} ${vertical})"/>`;
+    }).join('');
+
+    return `<g fill="url(#side)">${layers}</g>` +
+        `<use href="#${id}" fill="url(#${id}-face)"/>` +
+        `<use href="#${id}" fill="none" stroke="url(#rim)" stroke-width="3" stroke-linejoin="round"/>`;
+}
+
+function createLogoSvg(tone: PromptbookLogoTone): string {
+    const material = MATERIALS[tone];
+    const outlines = LOGO_FACES.map(({ id, outline }) => `<path id="${id}" d="${outline}"/>`).join('');
+    const faceGradients = LOGO_FACES.map(({ id }) => createGradient(`${id}-face`, material[id])).join('');
+
+    return '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n' +
+        '<!-- Generated by npm run generate-logo. Edit scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts. -->\n' +
+        `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${VIEW_BOX_SIZE}" height="${VIEW_BOX_SIZE}"` +
+        ` viewBox="0 0 ${VIEW_BOX_SIZE} ${VIEW_BOX_SIZE}" fill-rule="evenodd">` +
+        '<title>Promptbook 3D logo</title>' +
+        `<defs>${outlines}${faceGradients}${createGradient('side', material.side)}` +
+        `${createGradient('rim', material.rim)}</defs>${LOGO_FACES.map(createFace).join('')}</svg>\n`;
+}
+
+async function writeLogoAssets(tone: PromptbookLogoTone): Promise<void> {
+    const assets = PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS[tone];
+    const svg = createLogoSvg(tone);
+
+    await writeFile(path.join(OUTPUT_DIRECTORY, assets.svg), svg);
+    for (const [size, destination] of [[256, assets.png256], [1024, assets.png1024]] as const) {
+        await sharp(Buffer.from(svg), { density: 288 })
+            .resize(size, size)
+            .png()
+            .toFile(path.join(OUTPUT_DIRECTORY, destination));
+    }
+}
+
+async function main(): Promise<void> {
+    await writeLogoAssets('blue');
+    await writeLogoAssets('white');
+    console.info('Generated blue and white 3D Promptbook logos (SVG, 256 PNG, 1024 PNG).');
+}
+
+main().catch((error: unknown) => {
+    console.error(error);
+    process.exitCode = 1;
+});

/bin/zsh -lc "node --input-type=module <<'JAVASCRIPT'
import sharp from 'sharp';
import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
const SVG = await readFile('public/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-blue.svg','utf8');
const PATHS = [...SVG.matchAll(/<path id=\"["'^"]+" d="([''^"]+)"'"\\/>/g)].map(match => "'`<path d="${match[1]}"/>`).join('"'');
const FRONT = await sharp(Buffer.from("'`<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="1024" height="1024" fill-rule="evenodd">${PATHS}</svg>`)).ensureAlpha().raw().toBuffer();
const ORIGINAL = await sharp('"'public/logo/promptbook-logo-blue-transparent-1024.png').ensureAlpha().raw().toBuffer();
let intersection = 0;
let union = 0;
for (let index = 0; index < FRONT.length; index += 4) {
    const isOriginalInk = ORIGINAL[index + 3] > 128 && ORIGINAL[index] < 100;
    const isFrontInk = FRONT[index + 3] > 128;
    if (isOriginalInk && isFrontInk) intersection++;
    if (isOriginalInk || isFrontInk) union++;
}
console.log({ silhouetteIntersectionOverUnion: intersection / union, originalCornerPixel: Array.from(ORIGINAL.subarray(0,4)) });

diff --git a/README.md b/README.md
index edf7257a302096b486cd1cc6bba29c38d579ac24..c9a652e6c818ec60446911b94bac11703fcc339e
--- a/README.md
+++ b/README.md
@@ -71,6 +71,18 @@
 
 ## Technology
 
+### Promptbook logo
+
+The shared `PromptbookLogo` component uses a static 3D SVG, with a blue material for light surfaces and a white
+material for dark ones. The same artwork is available on `/branding` as SVG and transparent 256/1024-pixel PNGs,
+alongside the original flat logos. It needs no animation or WebGL runtime.
+
+Edit the measured outlines, extrusion and lighting in `scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts`, then run
+`npm run generate-logo` and commit the exports in `public/logo/`. Their public paths are shared through
+`lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets.ts`; the generator is an explicit development step, not part of page rendering.
+
+### Stack
+
 - **Next.js 15** App Router, React, and TypeScript
 - **Tailwind CSS** and Radix-based reusable UI components
 - **PostgreSQL** migrations and server-side access through `pg`

diff --git a/components/promptbook-logo.tsx b/components/promptbook-logo.tsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..2fcc49415664fbcd1a6937b83fe05f5a60cf5000
--- /dev/null
+++ b/components/promptbook-logo.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,33 @@
+import { PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS, type PromptbookLogoTone } from '@/lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets';
+import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
+import Image from 'next/image';
+
+interface PromptbookLogoProps {
+    /** The white material keeps its shading on dark backgrounds. */
+    tone?: PromptbookLogoTone;
+    size?: number;
+    className?: string;
+    /** Use an empty label when adjacent text already names Promptbook. */
+    alt?: string;
+    isPriority?: boolean;
+}
+
+/** The shared 3D mark is an ordinary image: crisp at any size, with no canvas, motion, or hydration dependency. */
+export function PromptbookLogo({
+    tone = 'blue',
+    size = 32,
+    className,
+    alt = 'Promptbook',
+    isPriority = false,
+}: PromptbookLogoProps) {
+    return (
+        <Image
+            src={PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS[tone].svg}
+            alt={alt}
+            width={size}
+            height={size}
+            className={cn('shrink-0 object-contain', className)}
+            priority={isPriority}
+        />
+    );
+}
diff --git a/lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets.ts b/lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..c30c10be5fdaf7dc4aed32868a859232e41adae3
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,15 @@
+/** Static exports of the same 3D artwork, generated by scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts. */
+export const PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS = {
+    blue: {
+        svg: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-blue.svg',
+        png256: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-blue-256.png',
+        png1024: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-blue-1024.png',
+    },
+    white: {
+        svg: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-white.svg',
+        png256: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-white-256.png',
+        png1024: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-white-1024.png',
+    },
+} as const;
+
+export type PromptbookLogoTone = keyof typeof PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS;

--- a/package.json

@@ -7,6 +7,7 @@

         "start": "next start",
         "lint": "next lint",

         "test": "vitest run",
         "test-e2e": "playwright test",
         "test-e2e:headed": "playwright test --headed",
diff --git a/scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts b/scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..b271ea5ce07287638c8b1be7d29133c72fa0d72b
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,118 @@
+/**
+ * Rebuilds the transparent 3D logo exports with `npm run generate-logo`.
+ *
+ * The front outlines are measured from public/logo/promptbook-logo-blue-transparent-1024.png.
+ * Its three disconnected pieces and the B counter are kept intact. Each face is extruded along its own
+ * isometric normal; material gradients and a narrow rim supply the lighting. Blue and white use the same geometry.
+ * Only this build-time script draws the artwork; pages and downloads share its static SVG and PNG files.
+ */
+import { writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import sharp from 'sharp';
+import { PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS, type PromptbookLogoTone } from '../lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets';
+
+const VIEW_BOX_SIZE = 1024;
+const DEPTH_STEPS = 36;
+const OUTPUT_DIRECTORY = path.resolve(process.cwd(), 'public');
+
+const LOGO_FACES = [
+    {
+        id: 'top',
+        outline: 'M60 283 467 47V283Z',
+        depth: [0, 32],
+    },
+    {
+        id: 'p',
+        outline:
+            'M184 395C181 393 179 395 179 401V768C179 775 180 779 187 783L222 802' +
+            'C224 803 226 803 226 800V638L274 666C311 688 335 672 346 642' +
+            'C354 619 354 582 346 548C336 505 312 469 279 448Z',
+        depth: [28, -16],
+    },
+    {
+        id: 'b',
+        outline:
+            'M470 520 878 285V755L470 989Z' +
+            'M588 498V878L661 836C723 801 760 746 760 675' +
+            'C760 636 748 609 719 605C744 576 757 542 757 501' +
+            'C757 456 741 421 705 438L592 492C589 494 588 495 588 498Z',
+        depth: [-28, -16],
+    },
+] as const;
+
+const MATERIALS = {
+    blue: {
+        top: ['#797bff', '#3434be', '#141077'],
+        p: ['#494cdb', '#211a9d', '#090548'],
+        b: ['#5b5bea', '#2420ac', '#09045b'],
+        side: ['#343598', '#080431'],
+        rim: ['#cedcff', '#777cea', '#272073'],
+    },
+    white: {
+        top: ['#ffffff', '#edf6ff', '#b5cde4'],
+        p: ['#f1f8ff', '#cfdfef', '#94acc9'],
+        b: ['#ffffff', '#e2effa', '#b7cfe5'],
+        side: ['#93aecb', '#4d6684'],
+        rim: ['#ffffff', '#ffffff', '#a8c6df'],
+    },
+} as const;
+
+function createGradient(id: string, colors: readonly string[]): string {
+    const stops = colors.map((color, index) =>
+        `<stop offset="${index / (colors.length - 1)}" stop-color="${color}"/>`,
+    ).join('');
+
+    return `<linearGradient id="${id}" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0.8" y2="1">${stops}</linearGradient>`;
+}
+
+function createFace({ id, depth }: (typeof LOGO_FACES)[number]): string {
+    const layers = Array.from({ length: DEPTH_STEPS }, (_, index) => {
+        const fraction = (DEPTH_STEPS - index) / DEPTH_STEPS;
+        const horizontal = (depth[0] * fraction).toFixed(2);
+        const vertical = (depth[1] * fraction).toFixed(2);
+
+        return `<use href="#${id}" transform="translate(${horizontal} ${vertical})"/>`;
+    }).join('');
+
+    return `<g fill="url(#side)">${layers}</g>` +
+        `<use href="#${id}" fill="url(#${id}-face)"/>` +
+        `<use href="#${id}" fill="none" stroke="url(#rim)" stroke-width="3" stroke-linejoin="round"/>`;
+}
+
+function createLogoSvg(tone: PromptbookLogoTone): string {
+    const material = MATERIALS[tone];
+    const outlines = LOGO_FACES.map(({ id, outline }) => `<path id="${id}" d="${outline}"/>`).join('');
+    const faceGradients = LOGO_FACES.map(({ id }) => createGradient(`${id}-face`, material[id])).join('');
+
+    return '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n' +
+        '<!-- Generated by npm run generate-logo. Edit scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts. -->\n' +
+        `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${VIEW_BOX_SIZE}" height="${VIEW_BOX_SIZE}"` +
+        ` viewBox="0 0 ${VIEW_BOX_SIZE} ${VIEW_BOX_SIZE}" fill-rule="evenodd">` +
+        '<title>Promptbook 3D logo</title>' +
+        `<defs>${outlines}${faceGradients}${createGradient('side', material.side)}` +
+        `${createGradient('rim', material.rim)}</defs>${LOGO_FACES.map(createFace).join('')}</svg>\n`;
+}
+
+async function writeLogoAssets(tone: PromptbookLogoTone): Promise<void> {
+    const assets = PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS[tone];
+    const svg = createLogoSvg(tone);
+
+    await writeFile(path.join(OUTPUT_DIRECTORY, assets.svg), svg);
+    for (const [size, destination] of [[256, assets.png256], [1024, assets.png1024]] as const) {
+        await sharp(Buffer.from(svg), { density: 288 })
+            .resize(size, size)
+            .png()
+            .toFile(path.join(OUTPUT_DIRECTORY, destination));
+    }
+}
+
+async function main(): Promise<void> {
+    await writeLogoAssets('blue');
+    await writeLogoAssets('white');
+    console.info('Generated blue and white 3D Promptbook logos (SVG, 256 PNG, 1024 PNG).');
+}
+
+main().catch((error: unknown) => {
+    console.error(error);
+    process.exitCode = 1;
+});

diff --git a/AGENTS.md b/AGENTS.md
index 3aa2eb0d340ef06f809de8fcc8f5be038b8ac5f3..f7227952d8f0cf9c4396ced75677c59aea5d6130
--- a/AGENTS.md
+++ b/AGENTS.md
@@ -8,6 +8,10 @@
 - `/` redirects to `/cs` or `/en` using `Accept-Language`.
 - `/cs` is the Czech homepage and source of truth for homepage structure and copy.
 - `/en` is its English localization.
+- Shared Promptbook headers, footers and both homepage heroes use `PromptbookLogo`, the static 3D mark.
+  Blue and white materials share one geometry in `scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts`; `npm run generate-logo`
+  rebuilds their transparent SVG and PNG exports. `/branding` previews and downloads the same files through
+  `lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets.ts`, alongside the original flat logos. Custom page identities keep their own marks.
 - `/pro-mesta`, `/pro-firmy`, `/for-agro`, `/for-industry`, `/ai-supervize`,
   `/hackathon-factory`, and `/pavol` are specialized landing pages. `/pavol`
   redirects to `/cs/pavol` or `/en/pavol`; the localized pages are Pavol Hejny's
diff --git a/README.md b/README.md
index edf7257a302096b486cd1cc6bba29c38d579ac24..c9a652e6c818ec60446911b94bac11703fcc339e
--- a/README.md
+++ b/README.md
@@ -71,6 +71,18 @@
 
 ## Technology
 
+### Promptbook logo
+
+The shared `PromptbookLogo` component uses a static 3D SVG, with a blue material for light surfaces and a white
+material for dark ones. The same artwork is available on `/branding` as SVG and transparent 256/1024-pixel PNGs,
+alongside the original flat logos. It needs no animation or WebGL runtime.
+
+Edit the measured outlines, extrusion and lighting in `scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts`, then run
+`npm run generate-logo` and commit the exports in `public/logo/`. Their public paths are shared through
+`lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets.ts`; the generator is an explicit development step, not part of page rendering.
+
+### Stack
+
 - **Next.js 15** App Router, React, and TypeScript
 - **Tailwind CSS** and Radix-based reusable UI components
 - **PostgreSQL** migrations and server-side access through `pg`

diff --git a/components/promptbook-logo.tsx b/components/promptbook-logo.tsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..2fcc49415664fbcd1a6937b83fe05f5a60cf5000
--- /dev/null
+++ b/components/promptbook-logo.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,33 @@
+import { PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS, type PromptbookLogoTone } from '@/lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets';
+import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
+import Image from 'next/image';
+
+interface PromptbookLogoProps {
+    /** The white material keeps its shading on dark backgrounds. */
+    tone?: PromptbookLogoTone;
+    size?: number;
+    className?: string;
+    /** Use an empty label when adjacent text already names Promptbook. */
+    alt?: string;
+    isPriority?: boolean;
+}
+
+/** The shared 3D mark is an ordinary image: crisp at any size, with no canvas, motion, or hydration dependency. */
+export function PromptbookLogo({
+    tone = 'blue',
+    size = 32,
+    className,
+    alt = 'Promptbook',
+    isPriority = false,
+}: PromptbookLogoProps) {
+    return (
+        <Image
+            src={PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS[tone].svg}
+            alt={alt}
+            width={size}
+            height={size}
+            className={cn('shrink-0 object-contain', className)}
+            priority={isPriority}
+        />
+    );
+}
diff --git a/lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets.ts b/lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..c30c10be5fdaf7dc4aed32868a859232e41adae3
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,15 @@
+/** Static exports of the same 3D artwork, generated by scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts. */
+export const PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS = {
+    blue: {
+        svg: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-blue.svg',
+        png256: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-blue-256.png',
+        png1024: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-blue-1024.png',
+    },
+    white: {
+        svg: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-white.svg',
+        png256: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-white-256.png',
+        png1024: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-white-1024.png',
+    },
+} as const;
+
+export type PromptbookLogoTone = keyof typeof PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS;

diff --git a/scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts b/scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..b271ea5ce07287638c8b1be7d29133c72fa0d72b
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,118 @@
+/**
+ * Rebuilds the transparent 3D logo exports with `npm run generate-logo`.
+ *
+ * The front outlines are measured from public/logo/promptbook-logo-blue-transparent-1024.png.
+ * Its three disconnected pieces and the B counter are kept intact. Each face is extruded along its own
+ * isometric normal; material gradients and a narrow rim supply the lighting. Blue and white use the same geometry.
+ * Only this build-time script draws the artwork; pages and downloads share its static SVG and PNG files.
+ */
+import { writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import sharp from 'sharp';
+import { PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS, type PromptbookLogoTone } from '../lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets';
+
+const VIEW_BOX_SIZE = 1024;
+const DEPTH_STEPS = 36;
+const OUTPUT_DIRECTORY = path.resolve(process.cwd(), 'public');
+
+const LOGO_FACES = [
+    {
+        id: 'top',
+        outline: 'M60 283 467 47V283Z',
+        depth: [0, 32],
+    },
+    {
+        id: 'p',
+        outline:
+            'M184 395C181 393 179 395 179 401V768C179 775 180 779 187 783L222 802' +
+            'C224 803 226 803 226 800V638L274 666C311 688 335 672 346 642' +
+            'C354 619 354 582 346 548C336 505 312 469 279 448Z',
+        depth: [28, -16],
+    },
+    {
+        id: 'b',
+        outline:
+            'M470 520 878 285V755L470 989Z' +
+            'M588 498V878L661 836C723 801 760 746 760 675' +
+            'C760 636 748 609 719 605C744 576 757 542 757 501' +
+            'C757 456 741 421 705 438L592 492C589 494 588 495 588 498Z',
+        depth: [-28, -16],
+    },
+] as const;
+
+const MATERIALS = {
+    blue: {
+        top: ['#797bff', '#3434be', '#141077'],
+        p: ['#494cdb', '#211a9d', '#090548'],
+        b: ['#5b5bea', '#2420ac', '#09045b'],
+        side: ['#343598', '#080431'],
+        rim: ['#cedcff', '#777cea', '#272073'],
+    },
+    white: {
+        top: ['#ffffff', '#edf6ff', '#b5cde4'],
+        p: ['#f1f8ff', '#cfdfef', '#94acc9'],
+        b: ['#ffffff', '#e2effa', '#b7cfe5'],
+        side: ['#93aecb', '#4d6684'],
+        rim: ['#ffffff', '#ffffff', '#a8c6df'],
+    },
+} as const;
+
+function createGradient(id: string, colors: readonly string[]): string {
+    const stops = colors.map((color, index) =>
+        `<stop offset="${index / (colors.length - 1)}" stop-color="${color}"/>`,
+    ).join('');
+
+    return `<linearGradient id="${id}" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0.8" y2="1">${stops}</linearGradient>`;
+}
+
+function createFace({ id, depth }: (typeof LOGO_FACES)[number]): string {
+    const layers = Array.from({ length: DEPTH_STEPS }, (_, index) => {
+        const fraction = (DEPTH_STEPS - index) / DEPTH_STEPS;
+        const horizontal = (depth[0] * fraction).toFixed(2);
+        const vertical = (depth[1] * fraction).toFixed(2);
+
+        return `<use href="#${id}" transform="translate(${horizontal} ${vertical})"/>`;
+    }).join('');
+
+    return `<g fill="url(#side)">${layers}</g>` +
+        `<use href="#${id}" fill="url(#${id}-face)"/>` +
+        `<use href="#${id}" fill="none" stroke="url(#rim)" stroke-width="3" stroke-linejoin="round"/>`;
+}
+
+function createLogoSvg(tone: PromptbookLogoTone): string {
+    const material = MATERIALS[tone];
+    const outlines = LOGO_FACES.map(({ id, outline }) => `<path id="${id}" d="${outline}"/>`).join('');
+    const faceGradients = LOGO_FACES.map(({ id }) => createGradient(`${id}-face`, material[id])).join('');
+
+    return '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n' +
+        '<!-- Generated by npm run generate-logo. Edit scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts. -->\n' +
+        `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${VIEW_BOX_SIZE}" height="${VIEW_BOX_SIZE}"` +
+        ` viewBox="0 0 ${VIEW_BOX_SIZE} ${VIEW_BOX_SIZE}" fill-rule="evenodd">` +
+        '<title>Promptbook 3D logo</title>' +
+        `<defs>${outlines}${faceGradients}${createGradient('side', material.side)}` +
+        `${createGradient('rim', material.rim)}</defs>${LOGO_FACES.map(createFace).join('')}</svg>\n`;
+}
+
+async function writeLogoAssets(tone: PromptbookLogoTone): Promise<void> {
+    const assets = PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS[tone];
+    const svg = createLogoSvg(tone);
+
+    await writeFile(path.join(OUTPUT_DIRECTORY, assets.svg), svg);
+    for (const [size, destination] of [[256, assets.png256], [1024, assets.png1024]] as const) {
+        await sharp(Buffer.from(svg), { density: 288 })
+            .resize(size, size)
+            .png()
+            .toFile(path.join(OUTPUT_DIRECTORY, destination));
+    }
+}
+
+async function main(): Promise<void> {
+    await writeLogoAssets('blue');
+    await writeLogoAssets('white');
+    console.info('Generated blue and white 3D Promptbook logos (SVG, 256 PNG, 1024 PNG).');
+}
+
+main().catch((error: unknown) => {
+    console.error(error);
+    process.exitCode = 1;
+});

codex
The logos look correct on desktop and mobile, and all six downloads work. The homepage and branding smoke tests passed. I also saw two browser errors during the broader page check; I’m tracing those before the final build check.

 need no full-memory buffer. Other browsers use a download fallback capped at 256 MiB to avoid exhausting memory.
 The studio adds no environment variables, uploads, server APIs, or database migrations.
 
+## Promptbook logo
+
+The shared `PromptbookLogo` component uses a static 3D SVG, with a blue material for light surfaces and a white
+material for dark ones. The same artwork is available on `/branding` as SVG and transparent 256/1024-pixel PNGs,
+alongside the original flat logos. It needs no animation or WebGL runtime.
+
+Edit the measured outlines, extrusion and lighting in `scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts`, then run
+`npm run generate-logo` and commit the exports in `public/logo/`. Their public paths are shared through
+`lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets.ts`; the generator is an explicit development step, not part of page rendering.
+
 ## Technology
 
 - **Next.js 15** App Router, React, and TypeScript

diff --git a/components/promptbook-logo.tsx b/components/promptbook-logo.tsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..2fcc49415664fbcd1a6937b83fe05f5a60cf5000
--- /dev/null
+++ b/components/promptbook-logo.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,33 @@
+import { PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS, type PromptbookLogoTone } from '@/lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets';
+import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
+import Image from 'next/image';
+
+interface PromptbookLogoProps {
+    /** The white material keeps its shading on dark backgrounds. */
+    tone?: PromptbookLogoTone;
+    size?: number;
+    className?: string;
+    /** Use an empty label when adjacent text already names Promptbook. */
+    alt?: string;
+    isPriority?: boolean;
+}
+
+/** The shared 3D mark is an ordinary image: crisp at any size, with no canvas, motion, or hydration dependency. */
+export function PromptbookLogo({
+    tone = 'blue',
+    size = 32,
+    className,
+    alt = 'Promptbook',
+    isPriority = false,
+}: PromptbookLogoProps) {
+    return (
+        <Image
+            src={PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS[tone].svg}
+            alt={alt}
+            width={size}
+            height={size}
+            className={cn('shrink-0 object-contain', className)}
+            priority={isPriority}
+        />
+    );
+}
diff --git a/lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets.ts b/lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..c30c10be5fdaf7dc4aed32868a859232e41adae3
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,15 @@
+/** Static exports of the same 3D artwork, generated by scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts. */
+export const PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS = {
+    blue: {
+        svg: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-blue.svg',
+        png256: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-blue-256.png',
+        png1024: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-blue-1024.png',
+    },
+    white: {
+        svg: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-white.svg',
+        png256: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-white-256.png',
+        png1024: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-white-1024.png',
+    },
+} as const;
+
+export type PromptbookLogoTone = keyof typeof PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS;

diff --git a/scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts b/scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..b271ea5ce07287638c8b1be7d29133c72fa0d72b
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,118 @@
+/**
+ * Rebuilds the transparent 3D logo exports with `npm run generate-logo`.
+ *
+ * The front outlines are measured from public/logo/promptbook-logo-blue-transparent-1024.png.
+ * Its three disconnected pieces and the B counter are kept intact. Each face is extruded along its own
+ * isometric normal; material gradients and a narrow rim supply the lighting. Blue and white use the same geometry.
+ * Only this build-time script draws the artwork; pages and downloads share its static SVG and PNG files.
+ */
+import { writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import sharp from 'sharp';
+import { PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS, type PromptbookLogoTone } from '../lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets';
+
+const VIEW_BOX_SIZE = 1024;
+const DEPTH_STEPS = 36;
+const OUTPUT_DIRECTORY = path.resolve(process.cwd(), 'public');
+
+const LOGO_FACES = [
+    {
+        id: 'top',
+        outline: 'M60 283 467 47V283Z',
+        depth: [0, 32],
+    },
+    {
+        id: 'p',
+        outline:
+            'M184 395C181 393 179 395 179 401V768C179 775 180 779 187 783L222 802' +
+            'C224 803 226 803 226 800V638L274 666C311 688 335 672 346 642' +
+            'C354 619 354 582 346 548C336 505 312 469 279 448Z',
+        depth: [28, -16],
+    },
+    {
+        id: 'b',
+        outline:
+            'M470 520 878 285V755L470 989Z' +
+            'M588 498V878L661 836C723 801 760 746 760 675' +
+            'C760 636 748 609 719 605C744 576 757 542 757 501' +
+            'C757 456 741 421 705 438L592 492C589 494 588 495 588 498Z',
+        depth: [-28, -16],
+    },
+] as const;
+
+const MATERIALS = {
+    blue: {
+        top: ['#797bff', '#3434be', '#141077'],
+        p: ['#494cdb', '#211a9d', '#090548'],
+        b: ['#5b5bea', '#2420ac', '#09045b'],
+        side: ['#343598', '#080431'],
+        rim: ['#cedcff', '#777cea', '#272073'],
+    },
+    white: {
+        top: ['#ffffff', '#edf6ff', '#b5cde4'],
+        p: ['#f1f8ff', '#cfdfef', '#94acc9'],
+        b: ['#ffffff', '#e2effa', '#b7cfe5'],
+        side: ['#93aecb', '#4d6684'],
+        rim: ['#ffffff', '#ffffff', '#a8c6df'],
+    },
+} as const;
+
+function createGradient(id: string, colors: readonly string[]): string {
+    const stops = colors.map((color, index) =>
+        `<stop offset="${index / (colors.length - 1)}" stop-color="${color}"/>`,
+    ).join('');
+
+    return `<linearGradient id="${id}" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0.8" y2="1">${stops}</linearGradient>`;
+}
+
+function createFace({ id, depth }: (typeof LOGO_FACES)[number]): string {
+    const layers = Array.from({ length: DEPTH_STEPS }, (_, index) => {
+        const fraction = (DEPTH_STEPS - index) / DEPTH_STEPS;
+        const horizontal = (depth[0] * fraction).toFixed(2);
+        const vertical = (depth[1] * fraction).toFixed(2);
+
+        return `<use href="#${id}" transform="translate(${horizontal} ${vertical})"/>`;
+    }).join('');
+
+    return `<g fill="url(#side)">${layers}</g>` +
+        `<use href="#${id}" fill="url(#${id}-face)"/>` +
+        `<use href="#${id}" fill="none" stroke="url(#rim)" stroke-width="3" stroke-linejoin="round"/>`;
+}
+
+function createLogoSvg(tone: PromptbookLogoTone): string {
+    const material = MATERIALS[tone];
+    const outlines = LOGO_FACES.map(({ id, outline }) => `<path id="${id}" d="${outline}"/>`).join('');
+    const faceGradients = LOGO_FACES.map(({ id }) => createGradient(`${id}-face`, material[id])).join('');
+
+    return '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n' +
+        '<!-- Generated by npm run generate-logo. Edit scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts. -->\n' +
+        `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${VIEW_BOX_SIZE}" height="${VIEW_BOX_SIZE}"` +
+        ` viewBox="0 0 ${VIEW_BOX_SIZE} ${VIEW_BOX_SIZE}" fill-rule="evenodd">` +
+        '<title>Promptbook 3D logo</title>' +
+        `<defs>${outlines}${faceGradients}${createGradient('side', material.side)}` +
+        `${createGradient('rim', material.rim)}</defs>${LOGO_FACES.map(createFace).join('')}</svg>\n`;
+}
+
+async function writeLogoAssets(tone: PromptbookLogoTone): Promise<void> {
+    const assets = PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS[tone];
+    const svg = createLogoSvg(tone);
+
+    await writeFile(path.join(OUTPUT_DIRECTORY, assets.svg), svg);
+    for (const [size, destination] of [[256, assets.png256], [1024, assets.png1024]] as const) {
+        await sharp(Buffer.from(svg), { density: 288 })
+            .resize(size, size)
+            .png()
+            .toFile(path.join(OUTPUT_DIRECTORY, destination));
+    }
+}
+
+async function main(): Promise<void> {
+    await writeLogoAssets('blue');
+    await writeLogoAssets('white');
+    console.info('Generated blue and white 3D Promptbook logos (SVG, 256 PNG, 1024 PNG).');
+}
+
+main().catch((error: unknown) => {
+    console.error(error);
+    process.exitCode = 1;
+});

 - `/en` is its English localization.
+- Shared Promptbook headers, footers and both homepage heroes use `PromptbookLogo`, the static 3D mark.
+  Blue and white materials share one geometry in `scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts`; `npm run generate-logo`
+  rebuilds their transparent SVG and PNG exports. `/branding` previews and downloads the same files through
+  `lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets.ts`, alongside the original flat logos. Custom page identities keep their own marks.
 - `/pro-mesta`, `/pro-firmy`, `/for-agro`, `/for-industry`, `/ai-supervize`,

index edf7257a302096b486cd1cc6bba29c38d579ac24..c08f8f99b4aa91366d07a975138112155e2cb253
--- a/README.md

@@ -69,6 +69,16 @@
 need no full-memory buffer. Other browsers use a download fallback capped at 256 MiB to avoid exhausting memory.
 The studio adds no environment variables, uploads, server APIs, or database migrations.
 
+## Promptbook logo
+
+The shared `PromptbookLogo` component uses a static 3D SVG, with a blue material for light surfaces and a white
+material for dark ones. The same artwork is available on `/branding` as SVG and transparent 256/1024-pixel PNGs,
+alongside the original flat logos. It needs no animation or WebGL runtime.
+
+Edit the measured outlines, extrusion and lighting in `scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts`, then run
+`npm run generate-logo` and commit the exports in `public/logo/`. Their public paths are shared through
+`lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets.ts`; the generator is an explicit development step, not part of page rendering.
+
 ## Technology
 
 - **Next.js 15** App Router, React, and TypeScript

diff --git a/components/promptbook-logo.tsx b/components/promptbook-logo.tsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..2fcc49415664fbcd1a6937b83fe05f5a60cf5000

@@ -0,0 +1,33 @@
+import { PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS, type PromptbookLogoTone } from '@/lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets';
+import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
+import Image from 'next/image';
+
+interface PromptbookLogoProps {
+    /** The white material keeps its shading on dark backgrounds. */
+    tone?: PromptbookLogoTone;
+    size?: number;
+    className?: string;
+    /** Use an empty label when adjacent text already names Promptbook. */
+    alt?: string;
+    isPriority?: boolean;
+}
+
+/** The shared 3D mark is an ordinary image: crisp at any size, with no canvas, motion, or hydration dependency. */
+export function PromptbookLogo({
+    tone = 'blue',
+    size = 32,
+    className,
+    alt = 'Promptbook',
+    isPriority = false,
+}: PromptbookLogoProps) {
+    return (
+        <Image
+            src={PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS[tone].svg}
+            alt={alt}
+            width={size}
+            height={size}
+            className={cn('shrink-0 object-contain', className)}
+            priority={isPriority}
+        />
+    );
+}

+/** Static exports of the same 3D artwork, generated by scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts. */
+export const PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS = {
+    blue: {
+        svg: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-blue.svg',
+        png256: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-blue-256.png',
+        png1024: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-blue-1024.png',
+    },
+    white: {
+        svg: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-white.svg',
+        png256: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-white-256.png',
+        png1024: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-white-1024.png',
+    },
+} as const;
+
+export type PromptbookLogoTone = keyof typeof PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS;

new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..b271ea5ce07287638c8b1be7d29133c72fa0d72b
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,118 @@
+/**
+ * Rebuilds the transparent 3D logo exports with `npm run generate-logo`.
+ *
+ * The front outlines are measured from public/logo/promptbook-logo-blue-transparent-1024.png.
+ * Its three disconnected pieces and the B counter are kept intact. Each face is extruded along its own
+ * isometric normal; material gradients and a narrow rim supply the lighting. Blue and white use the same geometry.
+ * Only this build-time script draws the artwork; pages and downloads share its static SVG and PNG files.
+ */
+import { writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import sharp from 'sharp';
+import { PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS, type PromptbookLogoTone } from '../lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets';
+
+const VIEW_BOX_SIZE = 1024;
+const DEPTH_STEPS = 36;
+const OUTPUT_DIRECTORY = path.resolve(process.cwd(), 'public');
+
+const LOGO_FACES = [
+    {
+        id: 'top',
+        outline: 'M60 283 467 47V283Z',
+        depth: [0, 32],
+    },
+    {
+        id: 'p',
+        outline:
+            'M184 395C181 393 179 395 179 401V768C179 775 180 779 187 783L222 802' +
+            'C224 803 226 803 226 800V638L274 666C311 688 335 672 346 642' +
+            'C354 619 354 582 346 548C336 505 312 469 279 448Z',
+        depth: [28, -16],
+    },
+    {
+        id: 'b',
+        outline:
+            'M470 520 878 285V755L470 989Z' +
+            'M588 498V878L661 836C723 801 760 746 760 675' +
+            'C760 636 748 609 719 605C744 576 757 542 757 501' +
+            'C757 456 741 421 705 438L592 492C589 494 588 495 588 498Z',
+        depth: [-28, -16],
+    },
+] as const;
+
+const MATERIALS = {
+    blue: {
+        top: ['#797bff', '#3434be', '#141077'],
+        p: ['#494cdb', '#211a9d', '#090548'],
+        b: ['#5b5bea', '#2420ac', '#09045b'],
+        side: ['#343598', '#080431'],
+        rim: ['#cedcff', '#777cea', '#272073'],
+    },
+    white: {
+        top: ['#ffffff', '#edf6ff', '#b5cde4'],
+        p: ['#f1f8ff', '#cfdfef', '#94acc9'],
+        b: ['#ffffff', '#e2effa', '#b7cfe5'],
+        side: ['#93aecb', '#4d6684'],
+        rim: ['#ffffff', '#ffffff', '#a8c6df'],
+    },
+} as const;
+
+function createGradient(id: string, colors: readonly string[]): string {
+    const stops = colors.map((color, index) =>
+        `<stop offset="${index / (colors.length - 1)}" stop-color="${color}"/>`,
+    ).join('');
+
+    return `<linearGradient id="${id}" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0.8" y2="1">${stops}</linearGradient>`;
+}
+
+function createFace({ id, depth }: (typeof LOGO_FACES)[number]): string {
+    const layers = Array.from({ length: DEPTH_STEPS }, (_, index) => {
+        const fraction = (DEPTH_STEPS - index) / DEPTH_STEPS;
+        const horizontal = (depth[0] * fraction).toFixed(2);
+        const vertical = (depth[1] * fraction).toFixed(2);
+
+        return `<use href="#${id}" transform="translate(${horizontal} ${vertical})"/>`;
+    }).join('');
+
+    return `<g fill="url(#side)">${layers}</g>` +
+        `<use href="#${id}" fill="url(#${id}-face)"/>` +
+        `<use href="#${id}" fill="none" stroke="url(#rim)" stroke-width="3" stroke-linejoin="round"/>`;
+}
+
+function createLogoSvg(tone: PromptbookLogoTone): string {
+    const material = MATERIALS[tone];
+    const outlines = LOGO_FACES.map(({ id, outline }) => `<path id="${id}" d="${outline}"/>`).join('');
+    const faceGradients = LOGO_FACES.map(({ id }) => createGradient(`${id}-face`, material[id])).join('');
+
+    return '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n' +
+        '<!-- Generated by npm run generate-logo. Edit scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts. -->\n' +
+        `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${VIEW_BOX_SIZE}" height="${VIEW_BOX_SIZE}"` +
+        ` viewBox="0 0 ${VIEW_BOX_SIZE} ${VIEW_BOX_SIZE}" fill-rule="evenodd">` +
+        '<title>Promptbook 3D logo</title>' +
+        `<defs>${outlines}${faceGradients}${createGradient('side', material.side)}` +
+        `${createGradient('rim', material.rim)}</defs>${LOGO_FACES.map(createFace).join('')}</svg>\n`;
+}
+
+async function writeLogoAssets(tone: PromptbookLogoTone): Promise<void> {
+    const assets = PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS[tone];
+    const svg = createLogoSvg(tone);
+
+    await writeFile(path.join(OUTPUT_DIRECTORY, assets.svg), svg);
+    for (const [size, destination] of [[256, assets.png256], [1024, assets.png1024]] as const) {
+        await sharp(Buffer.from(svg), { density: 288 })
+            .resize(size, size)
+            .png()
+            .toFile(path.join(OUTPUT_DIRECTORY, destination));
+    }
+}
+
+async function main(): Promise<void> {
+    await writeLogoAssets('blue');
+    await writeLogoAssets('white');
+    console.info('Generated blue and white 3D Promptbook logos (SVG, 256 PNG, 1024 PNG).');
+}
+
+main().catch((error: unknown) => {
+    console.error(error);
+    process.exitCode = 1;
+});

/**
 * Rebuilds the transparent 3D logo exports with `npm run generate-logo`.
 *
 * The front outlines are measured from public/logo/promptbook-logo-blue-transparent-1024.png.
 * Its three disconnected pieces and the B counter are kept intact. Each face is extruded along its own
 * isometric normal; material gradients and a narrow rim supply the lighting. Blue and white use the same geometry.
 * Only this build-time script draws the artwork; pages and downloads share its static SVG and PNG files.
 */
import { writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import path from 'node:path';
import sharp from 'sharp';
import { PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS, type PromptbookLogoTone } from '../lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets';

const VIEW_BOX_SIZE = 1024;
const DEPTH_STEPS = 36;
const OUTPUT_DIRECTORY = path.resolve(process.cwd(), 'public');

const LOGO_FACES = [
    {
        id: 'top',
        outline: 'M60 283 467 47V283Z',
        depth: [0, 32],
    },
    {
        id: 'p',
        outline:
            'M184 395C181 393 179 395 179 401V768C179 775 180 779 187 783L222 802' +
            'C224 803 226 803 226 800V638L274 666C311 688 335 672 346 642' +
            'C354 619 354 582 346 548C336 505 312 469 279 448Z',
        depth: [28, -16],
    },
    {
        id: 'b',
        outline:
            'M470 520 878 285V755L470 989Z' +
            'M588 498V878L661 836C723 801 760 746 760 675' +
            'C760 636 748 609 719 605C744 576 757 542 757 501' +
            'C757 456 741 421 705 438L592 492C589 494 588 495 588 498Z',
        depth: [-28, -16],
    },
] as const;

const MATERIALS = {
    blue: {
        top: ['#797bff', '#3434be', '#141077'],
        p: ['#494cdb', '#211a9d', '#090548'],
        b: ['#5b5bea', '#2420ac', '#09045b'],
        side: ['#343598', '#080431'],
        rim: ['#cedcff', '#777cea', '#272073'],
    },
    white: {
        top: ['#ffffff', '#edf6ff', '#b5cde4'],
        p: ['#f1f8ff', '#cfdfef', '#94acc9'],
        b: ['#ffffff', '#e2effa', '#b7cfe5'],
        side: ['#93aecb', '#4d6684'],
        rim: ['#ffffff', '#ffffff', '#a8c6df'],
    },
} as const;

function createGradient(id: string, colors: readonly string[]): string {
    const stops = colors.map((color, index) =>
        `<stop offset="${index / (colors.length - 1)}" stop-color="${color}"/>`,
    ).join('');

    return `<linearGradient id="${id}" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0.8" y2="1">${stops}</linearGradient>`;
}

function createFace({ id, depth }: (typeof LOGO_FACES)[number]): string {
    const layers = Array.from({ length: DEPTH_STEPS }, (_, index) => {
        const fraction = (DEPTH_STEPS - index) / DEPTH_STEPS;
        const horizontal = (depth[0] * fraction).toFixed(2);
        const vertical = (depth[1] * fraction).toFixed(2);

        return `<use href="#${id}" transform="translate(${horizontal} ${vertical})"/>`;
    }).join('');

    return `<g fill="url(#side)">${layers}</g>` +
        `<use href="#${id}" fill="url(#${id}-face)"/>` +
        `<use href="#${id}" fill="none" stroke="url(#rim)" stroke-width="3" stroke-linejoin="round"/>`;
}

function createLogoSvg(tone: PromptbookLogoTone): string {
    const material = MATERIALS[tone];
    const outlines = LOGO_FACES.map(({ id, outline }) => `<path id="${id}" d="${outline}"/>`).join('');
    const faceGradients = LOGO_FACES.map(({ id }) => createGradient(`${id}-face`, material[id])).join('');

    return '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n' +
        '<!-- Generated by npm run generate-logo. Edit scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts. -->\n' +
        `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${VIEW_BOX_SIZE}" height="${VIEW_BOX_SIZE}"` +
        ` viewBox="0 0 ${VIEW_BOX_SIZE} ${VIEW_BOX_SIZE}" fill-rule="evenodd">` +
        '<title>Promptbook 3D logo</title>' +
        `<defs>${outlines}${faceGradients}${createGradient('side', material.side)}` +
        `${createGradient('rim', material.rim)}</defs>${LOGO_FACES.map(createFace).join('')}</svg>\n`;
}

async function writeLogoAssets(tone: PromptbookLogoTone): Promise<void> {
    const assets = PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS[tone];
    const svg = createLogoSvg(tone);

    await writeFile(path.join(OUTPUT_DIRECTORY, assets.svg), svg);
    for (const [size, destination] of [[256, assets.png256], [1024, assets.png1024]] as const) {
        await sharp(Buffer.from(svg), { density: 288 })
            .resize(size, size)
            .png()
            .toFile(path.join(OUTPUT_DIRECTORY, destination));
    }
}

async function main(): Promise<void> {
    await writeLogoAssets('blue');
    await writeLogoAssets('white');
    console.info('Generated blue and white 3D Promptbook logos (SVG, 256 PNG, 1024 PNG).');
}

main().catch((error: unknown) => {
    console.error(error);
    process.exitCode = 1;
});
import { PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS, type PromptbookLogoTone } from '@/lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
import Image from 'next/image';

interface PromptbookLogoProps {
    /** The white material keeps its shading on dark backgrounds. */
    tone?: PromptbookLogoTone;
    size?: number;
    className?: string;
    /** Use an empty label when adjacent text already names Promptbook. */
    alt?: string;
    isPriority?: boolean;
}

/** The shared 3D mark is an ordinary image: crisp at any size, with no canvas, motion, or hydration dependency. */
export function PromptbookLogo({
    tone = 'blue',
    size = 32,
    className,
    alt = 'Promptbook',
    isPriority = false,
}: PromptbookLogoProps) {
    return (
        <Image
            src={PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS[tone].svg}
            alt={alt}
            width={size}
            height={size}
            className={cn('shrink-0 object-contain', className)}
            priority={isPriority}
        />
    );
}

 need no full-memory buffer. Other browsers use a download fallback capped at 256 MiB to avoid exhausting memory.
 The studio adds no environment variables, uploads, server APIs, or database migrations.
 
+## Promptbook logo
+
+The shared `PromptbookLogo` component uses a static 3D SVG, with a blue material for light surfaces and a white
+material for dark ones. The same artwork is available on `/branding` as SVG and transparent 256/1024-pixel PNGs,
+alongside the original flat logos. It needs no animation or WebGL runtime.
+
+Edit the measured outlines, extrusion and lighting in `scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts`, then run
+`npm run generate-logo` and commit the exports in `public/logo/`. Their public paths are shared through
+`lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets.ts`; the generator is an explicit development step, not part of page rendering.
+
 ## Technology
 
 - **Next.js 15** App Router, React, and TypeScript

diff --git a/components/promptbook-logo.tsx b/components/promptbook-logo.tsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..2fcc49415664fbcd1a6937b83fe05f5a60cf5000
--- /dev/null
+++ b/components/promptbook-logo.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,33 @@
+import { PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS, type PromptbookLogoTone } from '@/lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets';
+import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
+import Image from 'next/image';
+
+interface PromptbookLogoProps {
+    /** The white material keeps its shading on dark backgrounds. */
+    tone?: PromptbookLogoTone;
+    size?: number;
+    className?: string;
+    /** Use an empty label when adjacent text already names Promptbook. */
+    alt?: string;
+    isPriority?: boolean;
+}
+
+/** The shared 3D mark is an ordinary image: crisp at any size, with no canvas, motion, or hydration dependency. */
+export function PromptbookLogo({
+    tone = 'blue',
+    size = 32,
+    className,
+    alt = 'Promptbook',
+    isPriority = false,
+}: PromptbookLogoProps) {
+    return (
+        <Image
+            src={PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS[tone].svg}
+            alt={alt}
+            width={size}
+            height={size}
+            className={cn('shrink-0 object-contain', className)}
+            priority={isPriority}
+        />
+    );
+}
diff --git a/lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets.ts b/lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..c30c10be5fdaf7dc4aed32868a859232e41adae3
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,15 @@
+/** Static exports of the same 3D artwork, generated by scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts. */
+export const PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS = {
+    blue: {
+        svg: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-blue.svg',
+        png256: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-blue-256.png',
+        png1024: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-blue-1024.png',
+    },
+    white: {
+        svg: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-white.svg',
+        png256: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-white-256.png',
+        png1024: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-white-1024.png',
+    },
+} as const;
+
+export type PromptbookLogoTone = keyof typeof PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS;

diff --git a/scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts b/scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..b271ea5ce07287638c8b1be7d29133c72fa0d72b
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,118 @@
+/**
+ * Rebuilds the transparent 3D logo exports with `npm run generate-logo`.
+ *
+ * The front outlines are measured from public/logo/promptbook-logo-blue-transparent-1024.png.
+ * Its three disconnected pieces and the B counter are kept intact. Each face is extruded along its own
+ * isometric normal; material gradients and a narrow rim supply the lighting. Blue and white use the same geometry.
+ * Only this build-time script draws the artwork; pages and downloads share its static SVG and PNG files.
+ */
+import { writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import sharp from 'sharp';
+import { PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS, type PromptbookLogoTone } from '../lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets';
+
+const VIEW_BOX_SIZE = 1024;
+const DEPTH_STEPS = 36;
+const OUTPUT_DIRECTORY = path.resolve(process.cwd(), 'public');
+
+const LOGO_FACES = [
+    {
+        id: 'top',
+        outline: 'M60 283 467 47V283Z',
+        depth: [0, 32],
+    },
+    {
+        id: 'p',
+        outline:
+            'M184 395C181 393 179 395 179 401V768C179 775 180 779 187 783L222 802' +
+            'C224 803 226 803 226 800V638L274 666C311 688 335 672 346 642' +
+            'C354 619 354 582 346 548C336 505 312 469 279 448Z',
+        depth: [28, -16],
+    },
+    {
+        id: 'b',
+        outline:
+            'M470 520 878 285V755L470 989Z' +
+            'M588 498V878L661 836C723 801 760 746 760 675' +
+            'C760 636 748 609 719 605C744 576 757 542 757 501' +
+            'C757 456 741 421 705 438L592 492C589 494 588 495 588 498Z',
+        depth: [-28, -16],
+    },
+] as const;
+
+const MATERIALS = {
+    blue: {
+        top: ['#797bff', '#3434be', '#141077'],
+        p: ['#494cdb', '#211a9d', '#090548'],
+        b: ['#5b5bea', '#2420ac', '#09045b'],
+        side: ['#343598', '#080431'],
+        rim: ['#cedcff', '#777cea', '#272073'],
+    },
+    white: {
+        top: ['#ffffff', '#edf6ff', '#b5cde4'],
+        p: ['#f1f8ff', '#cfdfef', '#94acc9'],
+        b: ['#ffffff', '#e2effa', '#b7cfe5'],
+        side: ['#93aecb', '#4d6684'],
+        rim: ['#ffffff', '#ffffff', '#a8c6df'],
+    },
+} as const;
+
+function createGradient(id: string, colors: readonly string[]): string {
+    const stops = colors.map((color, index) =>
+        `<stop offset="${index / (colors.length - 1)}" stop-color="${color}"/>`,
+    ).join('');
+
+    return `<linearGradient id="${id}" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0.8" y2="1">${stops}</linearGradient>`;
+}
+
+function createFace({ id, depth }: (typeof LOGO_FACES)[number]): string {
+    const layers = Array.from({ length: DEPTH_STEPS }, (_, index) => {
+        const fraction = (DEPTH_STEPS - index) / DEPTH_STEPS;
+        const horizontal = (depth[0] * fraction).toFixed(2);
+        const vertical = (depth[1] * fraction).toFixed(2);
+
+        return `<use href="#${id}" transform="translate(${horizontal} ${vertical})"/>`;
+    }).join('');
+
+    return `<g fill="url(#side)">${layers}</g>` +
+        `<use href="#${id}" fill="url(#${id}-face)"/>` +
+        `<use href="#${id}" fill="none" stroke="url(#rim)" stroke-width="3" stroke-linejoin="round"/>`;
+}
+
+function createLogoSvg(tone: PromptbookLogoTone): string {
+    const material = MATERIALS[tone];
+    const outlines = LOGO_FACES.map(({ id, outline }) => `<path id="${id}" d="${outline}"/>`).join('');
+    const faceGradients = LOGO_FACES.map(({ id }) => createGradient(`${id}-face`, material[id])).join('');
+
+    return '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n' +
+        '<!-- Generated by npm run generate-logo. Edit scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts. -->\n' +
+        `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${VIEW_BOX_SIZE}" height="${VIEW_BOX_SIZE}"` +
+        ` viewBox="0 0 ${VIEW_BOX_SIZE} ${VIEW_BOX_SIZE}" fill-rule="evenodd">` +
+        '<title>Promptbook 3D logo</title>' +
+        `<defs>${outlines}${faceGradients}${createGradient('side', material.side)}` +
+        `${createGradient('rim', material.rim)}</defs>${LOGO_FACES.map(createFace).join('')}</svg>\n`;
+}
+
+async function writeLogoAssets(tone: PromptbookLogoTone): Promise<void> {
+    const assets = PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS[tone];
+    const svg = createLogoSvg(tone);
+
+    await writeFile(path.join(OUTPUT_DIRECTORY, assets.svg), svg);
+    for (const [size, destination] of [[256, assets.png256], [1024, assets.png1024]] as const) {
+        await sharp(Buffer.from(svg), { density: 288 })
+            .resize(size, size)
+            .png()
+            .toFile(path.join(OUTPUT_DIRECTORY, destination));
+    }
+}
+
+async function main(): Promise<void> {
+    await writeLogoAssets('blue');
+    await writeLogoAssets('white');
+    console.info('Generated blue and white 3D Promptbook logos (SVG, 256 PNG, 1024 PNG).');
+}
+
+main().catch((error: unknown) => {
+    console.error(error);
+    process.exitCode = 1;
+});

exec
/bin/zsh -lc "node --input-type=module <<'JAVASCRIPT'
import sharp from 'sharp';
import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
const SVG = await readFile('public/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-blue.svg','utf8');
const PATHS = [...SVG.matchAll(/<path id=\"["'^"]+" d="([''^"]+)"'"\\/>/g)].map(match => "'`<path d="${match[1]}"/>`).join('"'');
const FRONT = await sharp(Buffer.from("'`<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="1024" height="1024" fill-rule="evenodd">${PATHS}</svg>`)).ensureAlpha().raw().toBuffer();
const ORIGINAL = await sharp('"'public/logo/promptbook-logo-blue-transparent-1024.png').ensureAlpha().raw().toBuffer();
for (const [name, startX, startY, endX, endY] of [['top',0,0,1024,284],['p',0,320,439,900],['b',440,284,1024,1024]]) {
    let intersection = 0;
    let union = 0;
    for (let vertical=startY; vertical<endY; vertical++) for(let horizontal=startX; horizontal<endX; horizontal++) {
        const index=(vertical*1024+horizontal)*4;
        const isOriginalInk = ORIGINAL[index+3]>128 && ORIGINAL[index]<100;
        const isFrontInk = FRONT[index+3]>128;
        if(isOriginalInk && isFrontInk) intersection++;
        if(isOriginalInk || isFrontInk) union++;
    }
    console.log(name, intersection/union);
}

'use client';

import defaultBook from '@/businesses/_generic/default.book';
import dynamic from 'next/dynamic';

const BookEditor = dynamic(() => import('@promptbook/components').then((m) => m.BookEditor), { ssr: false });
const MockedChat = dynamic(() => import('@promptbook/components').then((m) => m.MockedChat), { ssr: false });

const SAMPLE_MESSAGES = [
    {
        sender: 'user',
        content: "Hello! Can you help me understand my company's leave policy?",
    },
    {
        sender: 'agent',
        content:
            'According to the company policy, employees get **20 days** of paid leave each year. Up to five unused days carry over. Do you want the details?',
    },
    {
        sender: 'user',
        content: 'What about sick leave?',
    },
    {
        sender: 'agent',
        content:
            "Sick leave is separate from annual leave. Employees get up to **10 days** of paid sick leave each year. A doctor's certificate is required after three days in a row.",
    },
];

const SAMPLE_PARTICIPANTS = [
    { name: 'user', fullname: 'You', isMe: true, color: '#6B7280' },
    { name: 'agent', fullname: 'Legal Assistant', color: '#30A8BD' },
];

export function BrandingComponentsDemo() {
    return (
        <div className="grid gap-8 lg:grid-cols-2">
            <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-[28px] border border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
                <div className="border-b border-slate-100 px-6 py-4">
                    <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-slate-500">Book editor</p>
                    <h3 className="mt-1 text-xl font-semibold text-slate-950">Write your AI agent in plain text</h3>
                </div>
                <BookEditor
                    value={defaultBook}
                    height="420px"
                    isReadonly
                    isDownloadButtonShown={false}
                    isAboutButtonShown={false}
                    isFullscreenButtonShown={false}
                    isBorderRadiusDisabled
                />
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-[28px] border border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
                <div className="border-b border-slate-100 px-6 py-4">
                    <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-slate-500">Mocked chat</p>
                    <h3 className="mt-1 text-xl font-semibold text-slate-950">Preview a conversation in your interface</h3>
                </div>
                <div className="h-[420px] overflow-hidden [&>*]:h-full [&>*]:max-w-full">
                    <MockedChat
                        messages={SAMPLE_MESSAGES}
                        participants={SAMPLE_PARTICIPANTS}
                        layout="STANDALONE"
                        isFocusedOnLoad={false}
                        isSaveButtonEnabled={false}
                        isCopyButtonEnabled={false}
                        isResettable={false}
                        isPausable={false}
                        delayConfig={{ showIntermediateMessages: 1 }}
                    />
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}

 - `/` redirects to `/cs` or `/en` using `Accept-Language`.

 - `/en` is its English localization.
+- Shared Promptbook headers, footers and both homepage heroes use `PromptbookLogo`, the static 3D mark.
+  Blue and white materials share one geometry in `scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts`; `npm run generate-logo`
+  rebuilds their transparent SVG and PNG exports. `/branding` previews and downloads the same files through
+  `lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets.ts`, alongside the original flat logos. Custom page identities keep their own marks.

 need no full-memory buffer. Other browsers use a download fallback capped at 256 MiB to avoid exhausting memory.
 The studio adds no environment variables, uploads, server APIs, or database migrations.
 
+## Promptbook logo
+
+The shared `PromptbookLogo` component uses a static 3D SVG, with a blue material for light surfaces and a white
+material for dark ones. The same artwork is available on `/branding` as SVG and transparent 256/1024-pixel PNGs,
+alongside the original flat logos. It needs no animation or WebGL runtime.
+
+Edit the measured outlines, extrusion and lighting in `scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts`, then run
+`npm run generate-logo` and commit the exports in `public/logo/`. Their public paths are shared through
+`lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets.ts`; the generator is an explicit development step, not part of page rendering.
+
 ## Technology
 
 - **Next.js 15** App Router, React, and TypeScript

+import { PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS, type PromptbookLogoTone } from '@/lib/branding/promptbookLogoAssets';
+import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
+import Image from 'next/image';
+
+interface PromptbookLogoProps {
+    /** The white material keeps its shading on dark backgrounds. */
+    tone?: PromptbookLogoTone;
+    size?: number;
+    className?: string;
+    /** Use an empty label when adjacent text already names Promptbook. */
+    alt?: string;
+    isPriority?: boolean;
+}
+
+/** The shared 3D mark is an ordinary image: crisp at any size, with no canvas, motion, or hydration dependency. */
+export function PromptbookLogo({
+    tone = 'blue',
+    size = 32,
+    className,
+    alt = 'Promptbook',
+    isPriority = false,
+}: PromptbookLogoProps) {
+    return (
+        <Image
+            src={PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS[tone].svg}
+            alt={alt}
+            width={size}
+            height={size}
+            className={cn('shrink-0 object-contain', className)}
+            priority={isPriority}
+        />
+    );
+}

+/** Static exports of the same 3D artwork, generated by scripts/generate-promptbook-logo.ts. */
+export const PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS = {
+    blue: {
+        svg: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-blue.svg',
+        png256: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-blue-256.png',
+        png1024: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-blue-1024.png',
+    },
+    white: {
+        svg: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-white.svg',
+        png256: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-white-256.png',
+        png1024: '/logo/promptbook-logo-3d-white-1024.png',
+    },
+} as const;
+
+export type PromptbookLogoTone = keyof typeof PROMPTBOOK_LOGO_ASSETS;
